_nemo-relay() {
    local i cur prev opts cmd
    COMPREPLY=()
    if [[ "${BASH_VERSINFO[0]}" -ge 4 ]]; then
        cur="$2"
    else
        cur="${COMP_WORDS[COMP_CWORD]}"
    fi
    prev="$3"
    cmd=""
    opts=""

    for i in "${COMP_WORDS[@]:0:COMP_CWORD}"
    do
        case "${cmd},${i}" in
            ",$1")
                cmd="nemo__relay"
                ;;
            nemo__relay,agents)

                ;;
        esac
    done

    case "${cmd}" in
        nemo__relay)
            opts="-h -V --config --bind --openai-base-url --anthropic-base-url --plugin-config-path --ready-file --max-hook-payload-bytes --max-passthrough-body-bytes --log-level --log-stderr-format --log-config-path --help --version claude codex pi mcp gateway config plugins install uninstall integrations model-pricing doctor agents completions run hook-forward help"
            if [[ ${cur} == -* || ${COMP_CWORD} -eq 1 ]] ; then
                COMPREPLY=( $(compgen -W "${opts}" -- "${cur}") )
                return 0
            fi
            case "${prev}" in
                --config)
                    COMPREPLY=($(compgen -f "${cur}"))
                    return 0
                    ;;
                --bind)
                    COMPREPLY=($(compgen -f "${cur}"))
                    return 0
                    ;;
                --openai-base-url)
                    COMPREPLY=($(compgen -f "${cur}"))
                    return 0
                    ;;

                    return 0

                    return 0
                    ;;

                    return 0
                    ;;

                    return 0

                    return 0
                    ;;

                    return 0
                    ;;
                *)
                    COMPREPLY=()
                    ;;
            esac
            COMPREPLY=( $(compgen -W "${opts}" -- "${cur}") )
            return 0
            ;;

        nemo__subcmd__relay__subcmd__config__subcmd__help__subcmd__edit)
            opts=""
            if [[ ${cur} == -* || ${COMP_CWORD} -eq 4 ]] ; then

                return 0
            fi
            case "${prev}" in
                *)
                    COMPREPLY=()
                    ;;
            esac
            COMPREPLY=( $(compgen -W "${opts}" -- "${cur}") )
            return 0
            ;;

            return 0
            ;;
        nemo__subcmd__relay__subcmd__uninstall)
            opts="-h --install-dir --force --dry-run --help codex claude-code pi all"
